# Endnotes and Source Index

Every fact and procedure in this book is tagged in the manuscript's source file with an
invisible Fact ID (`F-...`) or Procedure ID (`P-...`) HTML comment, tracing it to a real,
checked source. This index has two parts. Part 1 lists citations chapter by chapter, in
book order — useful for auditing a chapter's claims. Part 2 is a single alphabetical
index of every ID used anywhere in the book, with the chapter(s) that cite it — useful
for looking up one specific ID.

---
